  if(inRange&&P.shootCD<=0){
    cancelSmartAttack();
    attackQueued=true;
  }else{
    // Even if attack is cooling down, start approaching now and fire once ready.
    startSmartAttack(cur);
  }
}
let _combatAttackInputStamp=0;
function triggerAttackInput(e){
  if(e){
    try{e.preventDefault()}catch(_){}
    try{e.stopPropagation()}catch(_){}
  }
  const now=Date.now();

  // Android may emit pointerdown + touchstart + click for one tap.
  // Accept only one combat command from that group.
  if(now-_combatAttackInputStamp<170)return;
  _combatAttackInputStamp=now;
  queueAttack(e);
}
function attackPointerDown(e){
  try{bA.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId)}catch(_){}
  triggerAttackInput(e);
}
function attackPointerEnd(e){
  if(e){
    try{e.preventDefault()}catch(_){}
    try{e.stopPropagation()}catch(_){}
    try{if(bA.hasPointerCapture(e.pointerId))bA.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId)}catch(_){}
  }
  try{bA.blur()}catch(_){}
}
bA.addEventListener('pointerdown',attackPointerDown,{passive:false});
bA.addEventListener('touchstart',triggerAttackInput,{passive:false});
bA.addEventListener('touchend',triggerAttackInput,{passive:false});
bA.addEventListener('click',triggerAttackInput,{passive:false});
bA.addEventListener('pointerup',attackPointerEnd,{passive:false});
bA.addEventListener('pointercancel',attackPointerEnd,{passive:false});
bA.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>e.preventDefault());

function romanRank(n){return ['','I','II','III'][n]||''}
function updateSkillButtons(){
  var ck=classKeyFromName(P.cls),c=ck?GRIMOIRE_CATALOG[ck]:null;
  for(let i=0;i<4;i++){
    let el=document.getElementById('s'+(i+1));if(!el)continue;
    let sk=c&&c.active?c.active[i]:null,rank=sk?skillRank(sk.id):0;
    let left=sk?activeSkillCooldownLeft(sk.id):0;
    let va=sk?(GRIMOIRE_ART[sk.id]||{}):{};
    el.classList.toggle('skillLocked',rank<=0);
    el.classList.toggle('skillCooling',left>0);
    if(rank<=0){
      el.innerHTML='<span class="skillLock">🔒</span>';
    }else if(va.icon){
      el.innerHTML='<img class="skillHudImg" src="'+va.icon+'">'+
        (left>0?'<span class="skillCd">'+Math.ceil(left)+'</span>':'')+
        '<span class="skillRankTag">'+romanRank(rank)+'</span>';
    }else{
      el.innerHTML='<span>'+(sk.ic||String(i+1))+'</span>';
    }
    el.title=sk?(sk.n+(rank>0?' · ранг '+romanRank(rank):' · не изучен')):'Навык';
  }
}
function activeSkillCooldownLeft(id){
  var m=P.activeSkillReady||{},until=m[id]||0;
  return Math.max(0,(until-Date.now())/1000);
}
function setActiveSkillCooldown(id,sec){
  if(!P.activeSkillReady)P.activeSkillReady={};
  P.activeSkillReady[id]=Date.now()+sec*1000;
}
function playerSmokeActive(){
  return (P.smokeUntil||0)>Date.now();
}
function effectivePlayerDodge(){
  var bonus=playerSmokeActive()?(P.smokeDodgeBonus||0):0;
  return Math.min(90,(P.dodge||0)+bonus);
}

  applyPlayerVampirism(dmg,.6);
  if(e.isClanBoss){
    clanBossTrackDamage(dmg);
    showPickup('НАВЫК · −'+dmg,'#d9a7ff');
  }
  e.flash=8;e.aggro=true;
  for(var j=0;

function useLearnedHudSkill(slotIndex){
  var ck=classKeyFromName(P.cls),c=ck?GRIMOIRE_CATALOG[ck]:null;
  var sk=c&&c.active?c.active[slotIndex]:null;
  if(!sk)return;

  var rank=skillRank(sk.id);

  // HUD never learns or upgrades skills.
  if(rank<=0){
    showPickup('🔒 '+sk.n+' · изучается только во вкладке навыков','#b0a0c0');
    return;
  }

  // First combat pass: all 4 Assassin skills are now functional.
  if(ck==='assassin' && castAssassinSkill(sk,rank)){
    updateSkillButtons();
    return;
  }

  // Other classes keep their learned state, but are not silently mapped to old skills.
  showPickup(sk.n+' · эффект этого класса подключим отдельно','#c990ff');
}

const _combatSkillInputStamp=[0,0,0,0];
function triggerHudSkillInput(slotIndex,ev){
  if(ev){
    try{ev.preventDefault()}catch(_){}
    try{ev.stopPropagation()}catch(_){}
  }

  const now=Date.now();
  if(now-_combatSkillInputStamp[slotIndex]<170)return;
  _combatSkillInputStamp[slotIndex]=now;

  try{
    useLearnedHudSkill(slotIndex);
  }catch(err){
    console.error('HUD skill input failed',slotIndex,err);
    showPickup('НАВЫК · ошибка ввода','#ff9f9f');
  }
}

function v192RefreshSkillCooldowns(){
  try{
    const ck=classKeyFromName(P.cls),cat=ck?GRIMOIRE_CATALOG[ck]:null;
    for(let i=0;i<4;i++){
      const el=document.getElementById('s'+(i+1));if(!el)continue;
      const sk=cat&&cat.active?cat.active[i]:null,rank=sk?skillRank(sk.id):0;
      const left=sk?activeSkillCooldownLeft(sk.id):0;
      el.classList.toggle('skillLocked',rank<=0);
      el.classList.toggle('skillCooling',left>0);
      let cd=el.querySelector('.skillCd');
      if(rank>0&&left>0){
        if(!cd){cd=document.createElement('span');cd.className='skillCd';el.appendChild(cd)}
        const txt=String(Math.ceil(left));if(cd.textContent!==txt)cd.textContent=txt;
      }else if(cd){cd.remove()}
    }
  }catch(_){ }
}

// Dungeon population batches are cancellable when the player leaves the scene.
let dungeonSpawnToken=0;

function placePlayerAtDungeonSpawn(){
  jA=false;jX=0;jY=0;
  if(jS){jS.style.left='37px';jS.style.top='37px';}
  const sc=SCENES.dungeon;
  let sp;
  if(Number.isInteger(DUNGEON_TELEPORT_PENDING)&&DUNGEON_TELEPORT_PENDING>=0&&DUNGEON_TELEPORT_PENDING<DUNGEON_TELEPORT_ROUTES.length){
    sp=dungeonTeleportRouteWorld(DUNGEON_TELEPORT_PENDING);
    DUNGEON_TELEPORT_PENDING=null;
  }else{
    sp=dgNearestWalk(sc.startPos.x,sc.startPos.y);
  }
  P.scene='dungeon';
  P.x=sp.x;P.y=sp.y;
  P.vx=0;P.vy=0;
  P.dead=false;
  if(P.hp<=0)P.hp=P.mhp;
  if(P.mp<0)P.mp=0;
  P.tid=null;
  P.attacking=false;
  P.shootT=0;
  P.recoil=0;
  P.anim='idle';P.animFrame=0;P.animTimer=0;
  P.portalCD=60;
  return sp;
}

function clanSiegeLocalClanKey(){
  try{
    const c=CLAN_LOCAL_STATE&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.clan;
    if(!c)return '';
    return String(c.id||c.clanId||c.uid||c.name||'').trim().toLowerCase();
  }catch(_){return ''}
}
function clanSiegeTargetClanKey(t){
  if(!t)return '';
  return String(t.clanId||t.clanKey||t.clanName||(t.clan&&((t.clan.id||t.clan.name)))||'').trim().toLowerCase();
}

function ppaOnlineBadge(){
  let el=document.getElementById('ppaOnlineBadge');
  if(!el){
    el=document.createElement('div');el.id='ppaOnlineBadge';
    el.style.cssText='position:fixed;left:8px;top:8px;z-index:120;background:rgba(0,0,0,.68);border:1px solid rgba(120,210,255,.7);border-radius:7px;padding:5px 8px;color:#cceeff;font:bold 10px monospace;pointer-events:none;text-shadow:1px 1px 2px #000;display:none';
    document.body.appendChild(el);
  }
  return el;
}
function ppaOnlineSetStatus(text,col){
  const el=ppaOnlineBadge();text=String(text||'').trim();
  if(!text){el.textContent='';el.style.display='none';return}
  el.style.display='block';el.textContent=text;if(col)el.style.color=col;
}
function ppaOnlineProfileMeta(){
  let clanId='',clanName='';
  try{
    clanId=typeof clanSiegeLocalClanKey==='function'?(clanSiegeLocalClanKey()||''):'';
    clanName=(CLAN_LOCAL_STATE&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.clan&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.clan.name)||'';
  }catch(_){ }
  const name=String((INV&&INV.playerName)||PPA_ONLINE.selfName||'Игрок').slice(0,24);
  PPA_ONLINE.selfName=name;
  return{
    i:PPA_ONLINE.selfId,n:name,c:String(P.cls||'ГЕРОЙ').slice(0,20),
    g:String(clanId||'').slice(0,40),cn:String(clanName||'').slice(0,28),
    x:Math.round(P.x*10)/10,y:Math.round(P.y*10)/10,
    h:Math.max(0,Math.round(P.hp||0)),m:Math.max(1,Math.round(P.mhp||1)),
    f:P.face||1,a:String(P.anim||'idle').slice(0,12),t:Date.now()
  };
}
function ppaOnlineNormalizePacket(raw){
  raw=raw||{};
  return{
    id:String(raw.i||raw.id||''),
    name:String(raw.n||raw.name||'Игрок').slice(0,24),
    cls:String(raw.c||raw.cls||'ГЕРОЙ').slice(0,20),
    clanId:String(raw.g||raw.clanId||'').slice(0,40),
    clanName:String(raw.cn||raw.clanName||'').slice(0,28),
    x:Number(raw.x),y:Number(raw.y),hp:Number(raw.h!=null?raw.h:raw.hp),mhp:Number(raw.m!=null?raw.m:raw.mhp),
    face:Number(raw.f!=null?raw.f:raw.face)||1,anim:String(raw.a||raw.anim||'idle').slice(0,12),
    seq:Number(raw.q||raw.seq||0),ts:Number(raw.t||raw.ts||0)
  };
}
function ppaOnlineApplyPacket(raw,fromPresence){
  const p=ppaOnlineNormalizePacket(raw);if(!p.id||p.id===PPA_ONLINE.selfId)return;
  let r=PPA_ONLINE.remotes.get(p.id);
  const now=(typeof performance!=='undefined'&&performance.now)?performance.now():Date.now();
  if(!r){
    r={id:p.id,name:p.name,cls:p.cls,clanId:p.clanId,clanName:p.clanName,
      x:Number.isFinite(p.x)?p.x:0,y:Number.isFinite(p.y)?p.y:0,
      tx:Number.isFinite(p.x)?p.x:0,ty:Number.isFinite(p.y)?p.y:0,hasPos:Number.isFinite(p.x)&&Number.isFinite(p.y),
      hp:Number.isFinite(p.hp)?p.hp:0,mhp:Number.isFinite(p.mhp)&&p.mhp>0?p.mhp:1,
      face:p.face,anim:p.anim,lastSeq:p.seq||0,lastNetAt:now,lastDrawAt:now};
    PPA_ONLINE.remotes.set(p.id,r);
  }else{
    if(!fromPresence&&p.seq&&r.lastSeq&&p.seq<=r.lastSeq)return;
    if(p.name)r.name=p.name;if(p.cls)r.cls=p.cls;
    if(p.clanId!==undefined)r.clanId=p.clanId;if(p.clanName!==undefined)r.clanName=p.clanName;
    if(Number.isFinite(p.hp))r.hp=p.hp;if(Number.isFinite(p.mhp)&&p.mhp>0)r.mhp=p.mhp;
    r.face=p.face||r.face;r.anim=p.anim||r.anim;
    if(Number.isFinite(p.x)&&Number.isFinite(p.y)){
      if(!r.hasPos||Math.hypot(p.x-r.x,p.y-r.y)>520){r.x=p.x;r.y=p.y}
      r.tx=p.x;r.ty=p.y;r.hasPos=true;
    }
    if(p.seq)r.lastSeq=p.seq;
    r.lastNetAt=now;
  }
  if(!fromPresence)PPA_ONLINE.recvPackets++;
}

function stableItemArtIndex(it,len){
  if(!len)return 0;var uid=String((it&&(it.uid||it.name))||'');var seed=0;
  for(var i=0;i<uid.length;i++)seed=(seed+uid.charCodeAt(i)*(i+1))>>>0;
  return seed%len;
}

function simpleClassStatsAtLevel(key,lvl){
  const c=CLASS_BASE[key]||CLASS_BASE.assassin;
  const L=Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(lvl)||1));
  return {
    key:key,name:c.name,type:c.type,attackMode:c.attackMode,
    hp:c.hp+(L-1)*20,
    mp:c.mp+(L-1)*10,
    atk:c.atk,
    def:c.def_,
    spd:c.spd,
    atkSpd:c.atkSpd,
    range:c.range,
    crit:c.crit,
    dodge:c.dodge,
    critDmg:c.critDmg
  };
}
function applyClassBaseProfile(key,lvl){
  const s=simpleClassStatsAtLevel(key,lvl);
  P.cls=s.name;
  P.baseMhp=s.hp;P.baseMmp=s.mp;
  P.baseAtk=s.atk;P.baseDef=s.def;
  P.baseSp=s.spd;P.baseAtkSpd=s.atkSpd;
  P.baseRange=s.range;P.baseCrit=s.crit;
  P.baseDodge=s.dodge;P.baseCritDmg=s.critDmg;
  P.attackMode=s.attackMode;
  P.attackRange=s.range;
  P.sp=s.spd;
  return s;
}

function premiumClassChangeResult(ok,msg,targetKey){
  try{
    if(premiumShopFrame&&premiumShopFrame.contentWindow){
      premiumShopFrame.contentWindow.postMessage({
        type:'premiumClassChangeResult',
        ok:!!ok,message:String(msg||''),targetKey:targetKey||''
      },'*');
    }
  }catch(_){}
}

function premiumClassChangeItem(it,oldKey,newKey){
  if(!it||it.classKey!==oldKey||it.classKey==='all')return false;
  const slots=['weapon','helmet','armor','gloves','ring','legs','boots'];
  if(!slots.includes(it.slot))return false;

  const newName=(CLASS_ITEM_NAMES[newKey]&&CLASS_ITEM_NAMES[newKey][it.slot])||it.name||'Предмет';
  it.name=cleanItemName(newName);
  it.classKey=newKey;
  it.className=CLASS_DISPLAY[newKey]||newKey;
  it.img=classGearArt(it.rarity||'common',newKey,it.slot)||it.img||'';

  // Preserve everything the player already earned on this exact item:
  // rarity, level, enhancement, rolled stats, BM and UID.
  // Converted gear becomes account-bound to prevent class-change arbitrage.
  it.bound=true;
  it.tradeLocked=true;
  it.classChangeConverted=true;
  syncItemBM(it);
  return true;
}

function premiumConvertOwnedClassGear(oldKey,newKey){
  let converted=0;
  Object.keys(INV.equipped||{}).forEach(function(slot){
    if(premiumClassChangeItem(INV.equipped[slot],oldKey,newKey))converted++;
  });
  (INV.bag||[]).forEach(function(it){
    if(premiumClassChangeItem(it,oldKey,newKey))converted++;
  });

  // Only the player's own storages. Clan storage and auction lots are NEVER
  // transformed, otherwise class change could be abused as a market converter.
  if(INV.storage){
    ['personal','premium'].forEach(function(mode){
      (INV.storage[mode]||[]).for

  return s;
}

function premiumClassChangeResult(ok,msg,targetKey){
  try{
    if(premiumShopFrame&&premiumShopFrame.contentWindow){
      premiumShopFrame.contentWindow.postMessage({
        type:'premiumClassChangeResult',
        ok:!!ok,message:String(msg||''),targetKey:targetKey||''
      },'*');
    }
  }catch(_){}
}

function premiumClassChangeItem(it,oldKey,newKey){
  if(!it||it.classKey!==oldKey||it.classKey==='all')return false;
  const slots=['weapon','helmet','armor','gloves','ring','legs','boots'];
  if(!slots.includes(it.slot))return false;

  const newName=(CLASS_ITEM_NAMES[newKey]&&CLASS_ITEM_NAMES[newKey][it.slot])||it.name||'Предмет';
  it.name=cleanItemName(newName);
  it.classKey=newKey;
  it.className=CLASS_DISPLAY[newKey]||newKey;
  it.img=classGearArt(it.rarity||'common',newKey,it.slot)||it.img||'';

  // Preserve everything the player already earned on this exact item:
  // rarity, level, enhancement, rolled stats, BM and UID.
  // Converted gear becomes account-bound to prevent class-change arbitrage.
  it.bound=true;
  it.tradeLocked=true;
  it.classChangeConverted=true;
  syncItemBM(it);
  return true;
}

function premiumConvertOwnedClassGear(oldKey,newKey){
  let converted=0;
  Object.keys(INV.equipped||{}).forEach(function(slot){
    if(premiumClassChangeItem(INV.equipped[slot],oldKey,newKey))converted++;
  });
  (INV.bag||[]).forEach(function(it){
    if(premiumClassChangeItem(it,oldKey,newKey))converted++;
  });

  // Only the player's own storages. Clan storage and auction lots are NEVER
  // transformed, otherwise class change could be abused as a market converter.
  if(INV.storage){
    ['personal','premium'].forEach(function(mode){
      (INV.storage[mode]||[]).forEach(function(it){
        if(premiumClassChangeItem(it,oldKey,newKey))converted++;
      });
    });
  }
  return converted;
}

}

function premiumApplyTargetClass(targetKey){
  const c=PREMIUM_CLASS_CHANGE_STATS[targetKey];
  if(!c)return false;

  applyClassBaseProfile(targetKey,P.lvl);

  // Keep level / XP / rebirths. Return all earned stat points for a fresh
  // redistribution on the new class.
  const a=P.statAlloc||{};
  const refunded=(Number(P.statPts)||0)+Object.keys(a).reduce(function(sum,k){
    return sum+Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(a[k])||0));
  },0);
  P.statAlloc={hp:0,mp:0,atk:0,def:0,spd:0,atkspd:0,critdmg:0,crit:0,dodge:0};
  P.statPts=refunded;

  // Old class combat state never carries across.
  P.anim='idle';P.animFrame=0;P.animTimer=0;P.attacking=false;
  P.dir8=4;P.tid=null;P.shootT=0;P.recoil=0;
  PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length=0;
  PLAYER_ARROWS.length=0;
  P.skills=[];
  P.skillCd=[0,0,0,0];
  P.dmgMul=1;P.slowMul=1;P.chain=1;

  recomputeStats();
  P.hp=P.mhp;
  P.mp=P.mmp;
  return true;
}

function premiumChangeClass(targetKey){
  targetKey=String(targetKey||'').toLowerCase();
  const cfg=PREMIUM_CLASS_CHANGE_STATS[targetKey];
  if(!cfg){premiumClassChangeResult(false,'Класс не найден',targetKey);return}

  // Paid class change is a town service, not a combat switch.
  if(P.scene!=='safe'){
    premiumClassChangeResult(false,'Сменить класс можно только в Мирном городе',targetKey);return;
  }

  const oldKey=currentGearClassKey();
  if(oldKey===targetKey){
    premiumClassChangeResult(false,'Этот класс уже выбран',targetKey);return;
  }

  const have=Number(INV.gram)||0;
  if(have+1e-9<PREMIUM_CLASS_CHANGE_PRICE){
    premiumClassChangeResult(false,'Не хватает Gram · нужно '+PREMIUM_CLASS_CHANGE_PRICE,targetKey);return;
  }

  const oldName=CLASS_DISPLAY[oldKey]||P.cls||oldKey;
  const converted=premiumConvertOwnedClassGear(oldKey,targetKey);

  // 10 Gram is the service price. This is SPENDING, not a verified real-money
  // top-up, so it must not create auction slots.
  INV.gram=Math.round((have-PREMIUM_CLASS_CHANGE_PRICE)*10)/10;
  if(typeof recordGramSpend==='function')recordGramSpend(PREMIUM_CLASS_CHANGE_PRICE);

  premiumApplyTargetClass(targetKey);

  if(!INV.premiumShop)INV.premiumShop={purchasedBundles:{}};
  INV.premiumShop.lastClassChangeAt=Date.now();
  INV.premiumShop.classChangeCount=Math.max(0,Number(INV.premiumShop.classChangeCount)||0)+1;

  syncAllItemBM();
  saveGame();
  sendInvState();sendPremiumState();sendAuctionState();sendStorageState();sendClanState();
  updateUI();updateConsumableHud(true);

  const msg='Класс изменён: '+oldName+' → '+(CLASS_DISPLAY[targetKey]||cfg.name)+
    ' · экипировки преобразовано: '+converted;
  showPickup('⚔ '+msg+' · −'+PREMIUM_CLASS_CHANGE_PRICE+' Gram','#ffd16b');
  premiumClassChangeResult(true,msg,targetKey);
}

  return '';
}

function performRebirth(){
  if((P.lvl||1)<REBIRTH_LEVEL){
    showPickup('Перерождение доступно с 30 уровня','#ff9b72');
    return;
  }

  repairStatPointProgress();
  var oldLvl=Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(P.lvl)||1));

  // Only level growth is reset. Clean class stats are recalculated from lvl 1.
  // Gear, items, allocated points and free points stay.
  var _runeBeforeRebirth=runeUnlockedSlots();
  P.rebirths=(P.rebirths||0)+1;
  P.statPts=(P.statPts||0)+REBIRTH_BONUS_POINTS;
  P.lvl=1;
  P.xp=0;
  P.xn=xpRequiredForLevel(1);
  applyClassBaseProfile(classBaseKey(),1);

  recomputeStats();
  P.hp=P.mhp;P.mp=P.mmp;

  if(P._saved){
    P._saved.lvl=P.lvl;P._saved.xp=P.xp;P._saved.xn=P.xn;
    P._saved.rebirths=P.rebirths;
    P._saved.statPts=P.statPts;
    P._saved.statAlloc=Object.assign({},P.statAlloc);
  }

  saveGame();updateUI();sendInvState();
  showPickup('🔥 ПЕРЕРОЖДЕНИЕ '+P.rebirths+' · очки сохранены · +15'+(runeUnlockedSlots()>_runeBeforeRebirth?' · +1 слот руны':''),'#ffcc55');
}

function statsForPanel(){return {
  hp:Math.round(P.mhp),mp:Math.round(P.mmp),
  atk:Math.round((P.atk||0)*shopDamageMul()*10)/10,
  def:Math.round((P.def||0)*10)/10,
  spd:Math.round((P.spd||0)*shopSpeedMul()*100)/100,
  atkspd:Math.round((P.atkSpd||1)*shopAtkSpeedMul()*100)/100,
  range:Math.round(P.attackRange||95),
  critdmg:Math.round((P.critDmg||150)*10)/10,
  crit:Math.round((P.crit||0)*100)/100,
  dodge:Math.round((P.dodge||0)*100)/100,
  vampirism:Math.round((P.vampirism||0)*10)/10
};}

function equipFromBag(idx){
  var it=INV.bag[idx];if(!it)return;
  var ck=currentGearClassKey();
  if(it.classKey&&it.classKey!=='all'&&it.classKey!==ck){
    showPickup('Только для класса: '+(it.className||CLASS_DISPLAY[it.classKey]||it.classKey),'#ff7777');return;
  }
  INV.bag.splice(idx,1);
  var prev=INV.equipped[it.slot];
  INV.equipped[it.slot]=it;
  if(prev)INV.bag.push(prev);

  if(it.slot==='pet'){
    resetPetFollower();showPickup('🐾 Пет надет · автосбор лута включён','#8dff9a');
  }else if(it.slot==='wings'){
    showPickup('🪽 Крылья надеты','#c990ff');
  }else if(it.slot==='cloak'){
    showPickup('🧥 Плащ надет','#d6b176');
  }else if(it.slot==='artifact'){
    showPickup('🔮 Артефакт надет','#c990ff');
  }
  recomputeStats();
}
function unequipSlot(slot){

  INV.equipped[slot]=null;

}

function ppaCanRebuildItemArt(it){
  if(!it||!it.slot)return false;
  if(it.slot==='pet'){
    var pn=it.petName||it.name;
    return !!(pn&&typeof PET_DIR_ART!=='undefined'&&PET_DIR_ART[pn]);
  }
  if(it.slot==='wings'){
    return !!(typeof WING_DIR_ART!=='undefined'&&(WING_DIR_ART[it.rarity]||WING_DIR_ART.rare));
  }
  if(it.slot==='cloak'||it.slot==='artifact')return true;
  if(it.slot==='necklace')return true;
  return !!(typeof CLASS_ART_SLOTS!=='undefined'&&CLASS_ART_SLOTS[it.slot]&&it.classKey&&it.classKey!=='all');
}
function ppaCompactItemForSave(it){
  if(!it||typeof it!=='object')return it;
  var o=Object.assign({},it);
  if(ppaCanRebuildItemArt(o)){
    delete o.img;
    delete o.dirSprites;
    delete o.cardArt;
    delete o.iconArt;
  }
  return o;
}
function ppaCompactListForSave(a){
  return Array.isArray(a)?a.map(ppaCompactItemForSave):[];
}

  };
}

function ppaSaveLooksRich(o){
  if(!o||typeof o!=='object')return false;
  if(Array.isArray(o.bag)&&o.bag.length)return true;
  if(o.equipped&&Object.keys(o.equipped).some(function(k){return !!o.equipped[k]}))return true;
  if(o.storage&&Object.keys(o.storage).some(function(k){return Array.isArray(o.storage[k])&&o.storage[k].length}))return true;
  if(o.grimoires&&Object.keys(o.grimoires).some(function(k){return Number(o.grimoires[k])>0}))return true;
  if(o.skillRanks&&Object.keys(o.skillRanks).some(function(k){return Number(o.skillRanks[k])>0}))return true;
  if(Array.isArray(o.books)&&o.books.some(function(b){return b&&b.applied}))return true;
  return false;
}

// V257 account isolation: a valid character save may legitimately have an empty
// bag (for example a brand-new player or someone who sold/moved all gear).
// playerName/progression/currency make it an owned character state too.
function ppaSaveHasCharacterState(o){
  if(!o||typeof o!=='object')return false;
  if(ppaSaveLooksRich(o))return true;
  if(/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(String(o.playerName||'').trim()))return true;
  if(Number(o.lvl)>1||Number(o.xp)>0||Number(o.kil)>0||Number(o.rebirths)>0)return true;
  if(Number(o.gold)>0||Number(o.gram)>0||Number(o.ppa)>0||Number(o.arenaTokens)>0||Number(o.renameCards)>0)return true;
  if(o.materials&&Object.keys(o.materials).some(function(k){return Number(o.materials[k])>0}))return true;
  if(o.stones&&Object.keys(o.stones).some(function(k){return Number(o.stones[k])>0}))return true;
  if(o.runes&&Object.keys(o.runes).some(function(k){return Number(o.runes[k])>0}))return true;
  return false;
}

  if(!el){
    el=document.createElement('div');el.id='pickup';

    document.body.appendChild(el);
  }
  el.style.color=col;el.textContent=text;el.style.opacity='1';
  clearTimeout(el._t);el._t=setTimeout(()=>{el.style.opacity='0'},1200);
}

function applyClass(cls){
  if(!cls)return;

  const _classKey=classKeyFromName(cls.name)||'assassin';
  P.cls=CLASS_BASE[_classKey].name;
  P.anim='idle';P.animFrame=0;P.animTimer=0;P.attacking=false;
  P.dir8=4;
  PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length=0;
  PLAYER_ARROWS.length=0;
  cancelSmartAttack();

  // Persistent character only: restore the saved level/progression FIRST,
  // then calculate the selected class base at that level.
  if(P._saved&&P._saved.lvl)P.lvl=Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(P._saved.lvl)||1));
  applyClassBaseProfile(_classKey,P.lvl);

  if(P._saved){var sv=P._saved;
    if(sv.lvl)P.lvl=sv.lvl;
    if(sv.xp!=null)P.xp=sv.xp;
    if(sv.kil!=null)P.kil=sv.kil;
    P.xn=xpRequiredForLevel(P.lvl);
    if(typeof sv.statPts==='number')P.statPts=sv.statPts;
    else if(typeof sv.talentPts==='number')P.statPts=sv.talentPts;
    else P.statPts=10+Math.max(0,(P.lvl||1)-1)*3;
    P.statAlloc=Object.assign(
      {hp:0,mp:0,atk:0,def:0,spd:0,atkspd:0,critdmg:0,crit:0,dodge:0},
      sv.statAlloc||{}
    );
    P.rebirths=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(sv.rebirths)||0));
    repairStatPointProgress();
  }

  INV.books.forEach(function(b){
    if(b.applied){
      if(b.id==='fire')P.dmgMul=1.5;
      if(b.id==='ice')P.slowMul=0.7;
      if(b.id==='lightning')P.chain=2;
    }
  });

  P.sp=P.baseSp;
  recomputeStats();
  P.hp=P.mhp;
  P.mp=P.mmp;
  saveGame();
  updateUI();
}

function ppaClassPortraitKey(name){
  var n=String(name||'').trim().toUpperCase();
  return ({'СТРАЖ':'tank','ТАНК':'tank','БЕРСЕРК':'barbarian','ВАРВАР':'barbarian','ПАЛАДИН':'paladin','ГНОМ':'gnome','ГНОМ-КАНОНИР':'gnome','ЛУЧНИК':'archer','МАГ':'mage','АССАСИН':'assassin','УБИЙЦА':'assassin','ЖРЕЦ':'priest'})[n]||'assassin';
}

}
